POV: Lived With Your Parents Until 30 · image plan

One full cinematic image per phrase, timed to your voiceover file. Every noun the narrator says gets its own shot: say “keys”, see the keys; say “napkin”, see the napkin. The hero is always shiny metallic gold, and everyone else has plain porcelain-white skin. Every prompt is complete, with the style already at the end, so just press Copy and paste. Copying ticks the image as done.

12 min 17 s434 images1.7 s avg per image+ 10 cast sheetsstyle: cinematic semi-realistic
Rules for this video
  • Whole images only. No cut-outs, no layers, no empty backgrounds, no green screen, no parallax. Every image is a finished frame that goes straight on the timeline.
  • Hard cut on the word. Each card shows its start time and the words it covers. Put image 001 at 0:00 and cut to the next one at its start time. A slow 100→105% zoom is optional; nothing else moves.
  • Consistency. Make the 10 cast sheets first. On every image, attach the references in the Attach chips: the cast sheets for the people in the shot, plus the location master (the first wide shot of that place) so rooms stay identical.
  • Save each image with its number (001.png, 002.png…) so they sort in order in your editor. The timing sheet at the bottom lists every cut.
CastABCDEFGHITiming

1 · Cast sheets: make these first

These are reference sheets only. They never appear in the video; they just lock each face and outfit. Attach them whenever the chip names them.

CAST reference sheets
#C1 Cast sheet: Hero, age 22–29 new imagesheet
Used in 89 images
Character turnaround reference sheet of a young man in his early twenties, glossy polished metallic GOLD skin on his face, neck and hands, like a living gold statue with bright specular highlights and soft reflections, neat short dark-brown hair, a clean simple handsome face with calm dark eyes, clean-shaven; wearing a plain navy crew-neck sweater, grey chinos and white sneakers. Four full-body views side by side (front, three-quarter, side profile, back), same size, neutral standing pose, arms relaxed, plus one large close-up portrait of the face on the right. Plain soft-grey studio backdrop with a soft floor shadow. Style: cinematic semi-realistic digital illustration in the POV finance YouTube style: clean confident ink outlines, detailed painted backgrounds, movie-like composition, cinematic lighting and depth of field, rich natural colour grading, subtle film grain. The hero always has glossy metallic GOLD skin; every other person has smooth matte porcelain-white skin. 16:9.
